Data Type Conversions\n\n```python\n# Convert to numeric, forcing errors to NaN\ndf['column'] = pd.to_numeric(df['column'], errors='coerce')\n\n# Convert all columns except first to numeric\nfor col in df.columns[1:]:\n    df[col] = pd.to_numeric(df[col], errors='coerce')\n\n# Convert to string\ndf['column'] = df['column'].astype(str)\n\n# Convert to float with specific precision\ndf['column'] = df['column'].astype(float).round(2)\n```\n\n### 4. String Cleaning and Replacement\n\n```python\n# Replace commas with periods in all columns\ndf = df.replace(',', '.', regex=True)\n\n# Strip whitespace\ndf['column'] = df['column'].str.strip()\n\n# Replace specific patterns\ndf['column'] = df['column'].str.replace(r'\\s+', '', regex=True)\n\n# Apply to all string columns\nfor col in df.select_dtypes(include=['object']).columns:\n    df[col] = df[col].str.replace(',', '.')\n```\n\n### 5. Formatting Numbers\n\n```python\n# Round to 2 decimal places\ndf['column'] = df['column'].round(2)\n\n# Format as string with specific decimal places\ndf['column'] = df['column'].apply(lambda x: f'{x:.2f}' if pd.notnull(x) else x)\n\n# Apply formatting to all numeric columns\nnumeric_cols = df.select_dtypes(include=['float64', 'int64']).columns\nfor col in numeric_cols:\n    df[col] = df[col].apply(lambda x: f'{x:.2f}' if pd.notnull(x) else x)\n```\n\n### 6. Filtering and Validation\n\n```python\n# Filter rows based on conditions\ndf_filtered = df[df['column'] > threshold]\n\n# Filter with multiple conditions\ndf_filtered = df[(df['col1'] > min_val) & (df['col1'] < max_val)]\n\n# Remove outliers using IQR method\nQ1 = df['column'].quantile(0.25)\nQ3 = df['column'].quantile(0.75)\nIQR = Q3 - Q1\ndf_filtered = df[(df['column'] >= Q1 - 1.5*IQR) & (df['column'] <= Q3 + 1.5*IQR)]\n```\n\n### 7. Saving Clean Data\n\n```python\n# Save to CSV\ndf.to_csv('output.csv', index=False)\n\n# Save with specific float format\ndf.to_csv('output.csv', index=False, float_format='%.2f')\n\n# Save without index\ndf.to_csv('output.csv', index=False)\n```\n\n## Example: Complete Data Cleaning Pipeline\n\n```python\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\n\n# Read data\ndf = pd.read_csv('raw_data.csv')\n\n# Replace commas with periods (European decimal format)\ndf = df.replace(',', '.', regex=True)\n\n# Convert all columns except ID to numeric\nfor col in df.columns[1:]:\n    df[col] = pd.to_numeric(df[col], errors='coerce')\n\n# Remove rows with missing values\ndf_clean = df.dropna()\n\n# Round all numeric columns to 2 decimal places\nnumeric_cols = df_clean.select_dtypes(include=['float64']).columns\nfor col in numeric_cols:\n    df_clean[col] = df_clean[col].round(2)\n\n# Save cleaned data\ndf_clean.to_csv('cleaned_data.csv', index=False, float_format='%.2f')\n\nprint(f\"Original rows: {len(df)}\")\nprint(f\"Clean rows: {len(df_clean)}\")\nprint(f\"Removed rows: {len(df) - len(df_clean)}\")\n```\n\n## Best Practices\n\n1. **Always make a copy** before modifying data: `df_copy = df.copy()`\n2. **Check data types** after import: `df.dtypes`\n3. **Inspect data** before and after cleaning: `df.head()`, `df.describe()`\n4. **Log transformations** for reproducibility\n5. **Validate results** after each major transformation\n6. **Handle edge cases** like infinity, very large/small numbers\n7. **Use vectorized operations** instead of loops for better performance\n\n## Common Pitfalls\n\n- Not handling scientific notation properly before conversion\n- Forgetting to check for infinity values after division\n- Not preserving patient IDs when converting to numeric\n- Losing precision during type conversions\n- Not validating data ranges after cleaning\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

Kidney Function Tests\n\n#### Serum Creatinine\n- **US Conventional**: mg/dL\n- **SI Units**: µmol/L\n- **Conversion**: µmol/L ÷ 88.4 = mg/dL\n- **Reference Range**: 0.6-1.2 mg/dL (adults)\n- **CKD Range**: 1.5-20 mg/dL (varies by stage)\n\n#### Blood Urea Nitrogen (BUN)\n- **US Conventional**: mg/dL\n- **SI Units**: mmol/L\n- **Conversion**: mmol/L × 2.8 = mg/dL\n- **Reference Range**: 7-20 mg/dL\n- **CKD Range**: 20-200 mg/dL\n\n#### Cystatin C\n- **US Conventional**: mg/L\n- **SI Units**: mg/L (same)\n- **Reference Range**: 0.5-1.0 mg/L\n- **CKD Range**: 1.0-10 mg/L\n\n### 2. Electrolytes\n\n#### Sodium\n- **US Conventional**: mEq/L or mmol/L\n- **Reference Range**: 135-145 mEq/L\n- **CKD Range**: 115-170 mEq/L\n\n#### Potassium\n- **US Conventional**: mEq/L or mmol/L\n- **Reference Range**: 3.5-5.0 mEq/L\n- **CKD Range**: 2.0-8.0 mEq/L\n\n#### Chloride\n- **US Conventional**: mEq/L or mmol/L\n- **Reference Range**: 96-106 mEq/L\n- **CKD Range**: 75-140 mEq/L\n\n#### Magnesium\n- **US Conventional**: mg/dL\n- **SI Units**: mmol/L\n- **Conversion**: mmol/L × 2.43 = mg/dL\n- **Reference Range**: 1.7-2.2 mg/dL\n- **CKD Range**: 0.3-10 mg/dL\n\n### 3. Calcium and Bone Metabolism\n\n#### Serum Calcium (Total)\n- **US Conventional**: mg/dL\n- **SI Units**: mmol/L\n- **Conversion**: mmol/L × 4.0 = mg/dL\n- **Reference Range**: 8.5-10.5 mg/dL\n- **CKD Range**: 2.0-15 mg/dL\n\n#### Ionized Calcium\n- **US Conventional**: mg/dL\n- **SI Units**: mmol/L\n- **Conversion**: mmol/L × 4.0 = mg/dL\n- **Reference Range**: 4.5-5.3 mg/dL\n- **CKD Range**: 0.8-2.0 mg/dL\n\n#### Phosphorus\n- **US Conventional**: mg/dL\n- **SI Units**: mmol/L\n- **Conversion**: mmol/L × 3.1 = mg/dL\n- **Reference Range**: 2.5-4.5 mg/dL\n- **CKD Range**: 0.5-15 mg/dL\n\n### 4. Hematology\n\n#### Hemoglobin\n- **US Conventional**: g/dL\n- **SI Units**: g/L\n- **Conversion**: g/L ÷ 10 = g/dL\n- **Reference Range**: 12-16 g/dL (varies by sex)\n- **CKD Range**: 1.5-75 g/dL\n\n#### Hematocrit\n- **US Conventional**: % (percentage)\n- **SI Units**: fraction (0-1)\n- **Conversion**: fraction × 100 = %\n- **Reference Range**: 36-48%\n- **CKD Range**: 2-75%\n\n### 5. Iron Studies\n\n#### Serum Iron\n- **US Conventional**: µg/dL\n- **SI Units**: µmol/L\n- **Conversion**: µmol/L × 5.59 = µg/dL\n- **Reference Range**: 60-170 µg/dL\n- **CKD Range**: 2-300 µg/dL\n\n#### TIBC\n- **US Conventional**: µg/dL\n- **SI Units**: µmol/L\n- **Conversion**: µmol/L × 5.59 = µg/dL\n- **Reference Range**: 250-450 µg/dL\n- **CKD Range**: 10-600 µg/dL\n\n#### Ferritin\n- **US Conventional**: ng/mL\n- **SI Units**: µg/L (same value)\n- **Reference Range**: 20-200 ng/mL\n- **CKD Range**: 10-12000 ng/mL\n\n### 6. Proteins\n\n#### Albumin (Serum)\n- **US Conventional**: g/dL\n- **SI Units**: g/L\n- **Conversion**: g/L ÷ 10 = g/dL\n- **Reference Range**: 3.5-5.5 g/dL\n- **CKD Range**: 1.0-75 g/dL\n\n#### Total Protein\n- **US Conventional**: g/dL\n- **SI Units**: g/L\n- **Conversion**: g/L ÷ 10 = g/dL\n- **Reference Range**: 6.0-8.3 g/dL\n- **CKD Range**: 0.1-400 g/dL\n\n### 7. Lipids\n\n#### Cholesterol (Total, LDL, HDL)\n- **US Conventional**: mg/dL\n- **SI Units**: mmol/L\n- **Conversion**: mmol/L × 38.67 = mg/dL\n- **Reference Range**:\n  - Total: 125-200 mg/dL\n  - LDL: 0-100 mg/dL\n  - HDL: 40-60 mg/dL\n- **CKD Range**: 0.1-500 mg/dL\n\n#### Triglycerides\n- **US Conventional**: mg/dL\n- **SI Units**: mmol/L\n- **Conversion**: mmol/L × 88.57 = mg/dL\n- **Reference Range**: 0-150 mg/dL\n- **CKD Range**: 1-2000 mg/dL\n\n### 8. Glucose and Diabetes\n\n#### Glucose\n- **US Conventional**: mg/dL\n- **SI Units**: mmol/L\n- **Conversion**: mmol/L × 18.02 = mg/dL\n- **Reference Range**: 70-100 mg/dL (fasting)\n- **CKD Range**: 2-800 mg/dL\n\n### 9. Uric Acid\n\n#### Uric Acid\n- **US Conventional**: mg/dL\n- **SI Units**: µmol/L\n- **Conversion**: µmol/L ÷ 59.48 = mg/dL\n- **Reference Range**: 3.5-7.2 mg/dL\n- **CKD Range**: 0.1-20 mg/dL\n\n### 10. Bilirubin\n\n#### Total Bilirubin\n- **US Conventional**: mg/dL\n- **SI Units**: µmol/L\n- **Conversion**: µmol/L ÷ 17.1 = mg/dL\n- **Reference Range**: 0.1-1.2 mg/dL\n- **CKD Range**: 0.1-500 mg/dL\n\n## Python Implementation\n\n```python\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\n\nclass LabUnitConverter:\n    \"\"\"Convert clinical lab values to US conventional units\"\"\"\n\n    # Define conversion factors (multiply SI by factor to get US conventional)\n    CONVERSIONS = {\n        'Serum_Creatinine': {\n            'si_to_us': 1/88.4,  # µmol/L to mg/dL\n            'us_range': (0.6, 1.2),\n            'ckd_range': (1.5, 20)\n        },\n        'BUN': {\n            'si_to_us': 2.8,  # mmol/L to mg/dL\n            'us_range': (7, 20),\n            'ckd_range': (20, 200)\n        },\n        'Magnesium': {\n            'si_to_us': 2.43,  # mmol/L to mg/dL\n            'us_range': (1.7, 2.2),\n            'ckd_range': (0.3, 10)\n        },\n        'Serum_Calcium': {\n            'si_to_us': 4.0,  # mmol/L to mg/dL\n            'us_range': (8.5, 10.5),\n            'ckd_range': (2.0, 15)\n        },\n        'Ionized_Calcium': {\n            'si_to_us': 4.0,  # mmol/L to mg/dL\n            'us_range': (4.5, 5.3),\n            'ckd_range': (0.8, 2.0)\n        },\n        'Phosphorus': {\n            'si_to_us': 3.1,  # mmol/L to mg/dL\n            'us_range': (2.5, 4.5),\n            'ckd_range': (0.5, 15)\n        },\n        'Hemoglobin': {\n            'si_to_us': 0.1,  # g/L to g/dL\n            'us_range': (12, 16),\n            'ckd_range': (1.5, 75)\n        },\n        'Serum_Iron': {\n            'si_to_us': 5.59,  # µmol/L to µg/dL\n            'us_range': (60, 170),\n            'ckd_range': (2, 300)\n        },\n        'TIBC': {\n            'si_to_us': 5.59,  # µmol/L to µg/dL\n            'us_range': (250, 450),\n            'ckd_range': (10, 600)\n        },\n        'Albumin_Serum': {\n            'si_to_us': 0.1,  # g/L to g/dL\n            'us_range': (3.5, 5.5),\n            'ckd_range': (1.0, 75)\n        },\n        'Total_Protein': {\n            'si_to_us': 0.1,  # g/L to g/dL\n            'us_range': (6.0, 8.3),\n            'ckd_range': (0.1, 400)\n        },\n        'Total_Cholesterol': {\n            'si_to_us': 38.67,  # mmol/L to mg/dL\n            'us_range': (125, 200),\n            'ckd_range': (0.1, 500)\n        },\n        'LDL_Cholesterol': {\n            'si_to_us': 38.67,  # mmol/L to mg/dL\n            'us_range': (0, 100),\n            'ckd_range': (0.1, 500)\n        },\n        'HDL_Cholesterol': {\n            'si_to_us': 38.67,  # mmol/L to mg/dL\n            'us_range': (40, 60),\n            'ckd_range': (0.1, 200)\n        },\n        'Triglycerides': {\n            'si_to_us': 88.57,  # mmol/L to mg/dL\n            'us_range': (0, 150),\n            'ckd_range': (1, 2000)\n        },\n        'Glucose': {\n            'si_to_us': 18.02,  # mmol/L to mg/dL\n            'us_range': (70, 100),\n            'ckd_range': (2, 800)\n        },\n        'Uric_Acid': {\n            'si_to_us': 1/59.48,  # µmol/L to mg/dL\n            'us_range': (3.5, 7.2),\n            'ckd_range': (0.1, 20)\n        },\n        'Total_Bilirubin': {\n            'si_to_us': 1/17.1,  # µmol/L to mg/dL\n            'us_range': (0.1, 1.2),\n            'ckd_range': (0.1, 500)\n        }\n    }\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def needs_conversion(value, column, threshold_factor=1.5):\n        \"\"\"\n        Check if a value is likely in SI units and needs conversion.\n        Uses physiological ranges to detect unit mismatch.\n        \"\"\"\n        if column not in LabUnitConverter.CONVERSIONS:\n            return False\n\n        conv_info = LabUnitConverter.CONVERSIONS[column]\n        us_min, us_max = conv_info['ckd_range']\n\n        # If value is way outside expected range, likely wrong units\n        return value < (us_min / threshold_factor) or value > (us_max * threshold_factor)\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def convert_value(value, column):\n        \"\"\"Convert a value from SI to US conventional units\"\"\"\n        if column in LabUnitConverter.CONVERSIONS:\n            factor = LabUnitConverter.CONVERSIONS[column]['si_to_us']\n            return value * factor\n        return value\n```\n\n## Usage Example\n\n```python\nconverter = LabUnitConverter()\n\n# Check if value needs conversion\nif converter.needs_conversion(220, 'Serum_Creatinine'):\n    # 220 µmol/L is outside expected mg/dL range\n    converted = converter.convert_value(220, 'Serum_Creatinine')\n    print(f\"220 µmol/L = {converted:.2f} mg/dL\")  # 2.49 mg/dL\n\n# Process entire dataframe column\ndf['Serum_Creatinine'] = df['Serum_Creatinine'].apply(\n    lambda x: converter.convert_value(x, 'Serum_Creatinine')\n    if converter.needs_conversion(x, 'Serum_Creatinine') else x\n)\n```\n\n## Best Practices\n\n1. **Always validate ranges** after conversion\n2. **Log all conversions** for audit trail\n3. **Check for edge cases** (zero, negative values)\n4. **Consider clinical context** - some abnormal values are real\n5. **Document assumptions** about which unit system is being used\n6. **Test with known values** before batch processing\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1590,"cache_read_input_tokens":29007,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1590,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":18},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"57971aba-2aa9-494a-ab94-fad03f7137ec","uuid":"e6d67912-0326-43ee-8037-0c16f0736f3b"}

Scientific Notation\n\n**Problem**: Values like `1.2345e+02`, `7.6160e+00` need to be converted to normal decimal format.\n\n**Solutions**:\n\n```python\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\n\n# Method 1: Using pandas to_numeric with string conversion\ndf['column'] = pd.to_numeric(df['column'], errors='coerce')\n\n# Method 2: Force string conversion then to float\ndf['column'] = df['column'].astype(str).astype(float)\n\n# Method 3: Read CSV with float_precision parameter\ndf = pd.read_csv('file.csv', float_precision='high')\n\n# Method 4: Custom function to handle scientific notation\ndef normalize_scientific(value):\n    \"\"\"Convert scientific notation to normal decimal\"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return value\n    try:\n        # Convert to float (handles scientific notation automatically)\n        return float(value)\n    except:\n        return np.nan\n\ndf['column'] = df['column'].apply(normalize_scientific)\n```\n\n### 2. Comma as Decimal Separator\n\n**Problem**: European format uses comma for decimals: `12,34` should be `12.34`\n\n**Solutions**:\n\n```python\n# Method 1: String replacement before conversion\ndf = df.replace(',', '.', regex=True)\n\n# Method 2: Apply to specific columns\nfor col in df.columns[1:]:  # Skip ID column\n    if df[col].dtype == 'object':\n        df[col] = df[col].str.replace(',', '.')\n\n# Method 3: During CSV reading (less reliable)\ndf = pd.read_csv('file.csv', decimal=',')\n\n# Method 4: Custom function for mixed formats\ndef parse_number(value):\n    \"\"\"Handle both comma and period decimals\"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value) or value == '':\n        return np.nan\n\n    value = str(value).strip()\n\n    # Replace comma with period\n    value = value.replace(',', '.')\n\n    try:\n        return float(value)\n    except:\n        return np.nan\n\ndf['column'] = df['column'].apply(parse_number)\n```\n\n### 3. Inconsistent Decimal Places\n\n**Problem**: Values have different precision: `12.3`, `12.34567`, `12`\n\n**Solutions**:\n\n```python\n# Method 1: Round to specific decimal places\ndf['column'] = df['column'].round(2)\n\n# Method 2: Format as string with fixed decimals\ndf['column'] = df['column'].apply(lambda x: f'{x:.2f}' if pd.notnull(x) else x)\n\n# Method 3: Using numpy round\ndf['column'] = np.round(df['column'], 2)\n\n# Method 4: Apply to all numeric columns\nnumeric_cols = df.select_dtypes(include=[np.number]).columns\ndf[numeric_cols] = df[numeric_cols].round(2)\n```\n\n### 4. Thousands Separators\n\n**Problem**: Values like `1,234.56` or `1.234,56`\n\n**Solutions**:\n\n```python\n# Method 1: Remove thousands separators (US format: comma)\ndef clean_thousands_us(value):\n    \"\"\"Remove comma thousands separators\"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return value\n    value = str(value).replace(',', '')\n    return float(value)\n\n# Method 2: Handle European format (period as thousands, comma as decimal)\ndef clean_thousands_eu(value):\n    \"\"\"Handle European number format\"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return value\n    value = str(value)\n    # Remove period (thousands separator)\n    value = value.replace('.', '', value.count('.') - 1) if value.count('.') > 1 else value\n    # Replace comma (decimal) with period\n    value = value.replace(',', '.')\n    return float(value)\n\n# Method 3: Detect format automatically\ndef smart_number_parse(value):\n    \"\"\"Intelligently parse numbers with different formats\"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value) or value == '':\n        return np.nan\n\n    value = str(value).strip()\n\n    # Count periods and commas\n    period_count = value.count('.')\n    comma_count = value.count(',')\n\n    # No separators\n    if period_count == 0 and comma_count == 0:\n        return float(value)\n\n    # Only commas - could be thousands or decimal\n    if period_count == 0 and comma_count > 0:\n        if comma_count == 1 and len(value.split(',')[1]) <= 2:\n            # Likely decimal separator: 12,34\n            return float(value.replace(',', '.'))\n        else:\n            # Likely thousands: 1,234 or 1,234,567\n            return float(value.replace(',', ''))\n\n    # Only periods - could be thousands or decimal\n    if comma_count == 0 and period_count > 0:\n        if period_count == 1:\n            # Standard decimal: 12.34\n            return float(value)\n        else:\n            # European thousands: 1.234.567,89 (but no comma, unusual)\n            return float(value.replace('.', ''))\n\n    # Both separators - determine which is which\n    last_period_pos = value.rfind('.')\n    last_comma_pos = value.rfind(',')\n\n    if last_period_pos > last_comma_pos:\n        # Period is decimal: 1,234.56 (US format)\n        value = value.replace(',', '')\n        return float(value)\n    else:\n        # Comma is decimal: 1.234,56 (EU format)\n        value = value.replace('.', '').replace(',', '.')\n        return float(value)\n```\n\n## Complete Normalization Pipeline\n\n```python\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\n\ndef normalize_numeric_formats(df, id_column='patient_id', decimal_places=2):\n    \"\"\"\n    Normalize all numeric formats in a dataframe\n\n    Args:\n        df: Input dataframe\n        id_column: Column to exclude from conversion (usually ID)\n        decimal_places: Number of decimal places for output\n\n    Returns:\n        Cleaned dataframe with normalized numeric values\n    \"\"\"\n    # Make a copy to avoid modifying original\n    df_clean = df.copy()\n\n    # Get all columns except ID\n    numeric_columns = [col for col in df_clean.columns if col != id_column]\n\n    # Step 1: Replace commas with periods globally\n    # This handles the most common case: comma as decimal separator\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        if df_clean[col].dtype == 'object':\n            df_clean[col] = df_clean[col].astype(str).str.replace(',', '.')\n\n    # Step 2: Convert to numeric (handles scientific notation automatically)\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        df_clean[col] = pd.to_numeric(df_clean[col], errors='coerce')\n\n    # Step 3: Round to specified decimal places\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        df_clean[col] = df_clean[col].round(decimal_places)\n\n    return df_clean\n\n\ndef format_output(df, id_column='patient_id', decimal_places=2):\n    \"\"\"\n    Format numeric values as strings with fixed decimal places\n\n    Args:\n        df: Input dataframe with numeric values\n        id_column: Column to exclude from formatting\n        decimal_places: Number of decimal places\n\n    Returns:\n        Dataframe with formatted string values\n    \"\"\"\n    df_formatted = df.copy()\n\n    numeric_columns = [col for col in df_formatted.columns if col != id_column]\n\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        # Format each value as X.XX\n        df_formatted[col] = df_formatted[col].apply(\n            lambda x: f'{x:.{decimal_places}f}' if pd.notnull(x) else x\n        )\n\n    return df_formatted\n\n\n# Usage Example\ndef clean_clinical_data(input_file, output_file):\n    \"\"\"Complete pipeline for cleaning clinical data\"\"\"\n\n    # Read data\n    df = pd.read_csv(input_file)\n\n    print(f\"Original shape: {df.shape}\")\n    print(f\"Original missing values: {df.isnull().sum().sum()}\")\n\n    # Normalize formats\n    df_normalized = normalize_numeric_formats(df)\n\n    # Remove rows with missing values\n    df_clean = df_normalized.dropna()\n\n    print(f\"After removing missing: {df_clean.shape}\")\n    print(f\"Removed {len(df) - len(df_clean)} rows\")\n\n    # Format output with fixed decimals\n    df_output = format_output(df_clean)\n\n    # Save\n    df_output.to_csv(output_file, index=False)\n\n    print(f\"Saved to {output_file}\")\n\n    return df_output\n```\n\n## Validation Functions\n\n```python\ndef validate_numeric_format(df, decimal_places=2):\n    \"\"\"Validate that all numeric values are properly formatted\"\"\"\n\n    issues = []\n\n    for col in df.columns[1:]:  # Skip ID\n        for idx, val in df[col].items():\n            if pd.notnull(val):\n                val_str = str(val)\n\n                # Check for scientific notation\n                if 'e' in val_str.lower():\n                    issues.append(f\"Row {idx}, Col {col}: Scientific notation found: {val}\")\n\n                # Check for commas\n                if ',' in val_str:\n                    issues.append(f\"Row {idx}, Col {col}: Comma found: {val}\")\n\n                # Check decimal places\n                if '.' in val_str:\n                    decimals = len(val_str.split('.')[1])\n                    if decimals != decimal_places:\n                        issues.append(f\"Row {idx}, Col {col}: Wrong decimal places: {val}\")\n\n    if issues:\n        print(f\"Found {len(issues)} formatting issues:\")\n        for issue in issues[:10]:  # Show first 10\n            print(f\"  {issue}\")\n    else:\n        print(\"All values properly formatted!\")\n\n    return len(issues) == 0\n\n\ndef check_for_outliers(df, column, ckd_range):\n    \"\"\"Check if values are within expected physiological range\"\"\"\n    min_val, max_val = ckd_range\n\n    outliers = df[(df[column] < min_val) | (df[column] > max_val)]\n\n    if len(outliers) > 0:\n        print(f\"{column}: {len(outliers)} values outside range ({min_val}-{max_val})\")\n        print(f\"  Min: {df[column].min()}, Max: {df[column].max()}\")\n        return False\n\n    return True\n```\n\n## Best Practices\n\n1. **Always preserve original data** - work on copies\n2. **Log all conversions** - track what changed\n3. **Validate after each step** - catch errors early\n4. **Test with sample data first** - verify logic\n5. **Handle edge cases**:\n   - Empty strings\n   - Null/NaN values\n   - Infinity values\n   - Very large/small numbers\n6. **Document assumptions** about input format\n7. **Use consistent rounding** - avoid floating point errors\n\n## Common Pitfalls\n\n- Converting ID columns to numeric (losing leading zeros)\n- Not handling mixed formats in same column\n- Rounding too early (accumulating errors)\n- Not checking for infinity after operations\n- Assuming single format across all data\n- Not validating output format matches requirements\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3951,"cache_read_input_tokens":30597,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":3951,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":18},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"57971aba-2aa9-494a-ab94-fad03f7137ec","uuid":"74580d54-0a9b-4aa9-949b-edb80fc9c011"}

Skip ID\n        for idx, val in df[col].items():\n            if pd.notnull(val):\n                val_str = str(val)\n\n                # Check for scientific notation\n                if 'e' in val_str.lower():\n                    issues.append(f\"Row {idx}, Col {col}: Scientific notation found: {val}\")\n\n                # Check for commas\n                if ',' in val_str:\n                    issues.append(f\"Row {idx}, Col {col}: Comma found: {val}\")\n\n                # Check decimal places\n                if '.' in val_str:\n                    decimals = len(val_str.split('.')[1])\n                    if decimals != decimal_places:\n                        issues.append(f\"Row {idx}, Col {col}: Wrong decimal places: {val}\")\n\n    if issues:\n        print(f\"Found {len(issues)} formatting issues:\")\n        for issue in issues[:10]:  # Show first 10\n            print(f\"  {issue}\")\n    else:\n        print(\"All values properly formatted!\")\n\n    return len(issues) == 0\n\n\ndef check_for_outliers(df, column, ckd_range):\n    \"\"\"Check if values are within expected physiological range\"\"\"\n    min_val, max_val = ckd_range\n\n    outliers = df[(df[column] < min_val) | (df[column] > max_val)]\n\n    if len(outliers) > 0:\n        print(f\"{column}: {len(outliers)} values outside range ({min_val}-{max_val})\")\n        print(f\"  Min: {df[column].min()}, Max: {df[column].max()}\")\n        return False\n\n    return True\n```\n\n## Best Practices\n\n1. **Always preserve original data** - work on copies\n2. **Log all conversions** - track what changed\n3. **Validate after each step** - catch errors early\n4. **Test with sample data first** - verify logic\n5. **Handle edge cases**:\n   - Empty strings\n   - Null/NaN values\n   - Infinity values\n   - Very large/small numbers\n6. **Document assumptions** about input format\n7. **Use consistent rounding** - avoid floating point errors\n\n## Common Pitfalls\n\n- Converting ID columns to numeric (losing leading zeros)\n- Not handling mixed formats in same column\n- Rounding too early (accumulating errors)\n- Not checking for infinity after operations\n- Assuming single format across all data\n- Not validating output format matches requirements\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

\"\"\"Normalize numeric formats\"\"\"\n        logger.info(\"Step 2: Normalizing formats...\")\n        self.df_normalized = self.df_raw.copy()\n\n        # Get numeric columns (all except ID)\n      return self.df_normalized\n\n    def step3_harmonize_units(self):\n        \"\"\"Identify and convert values with wrong units\"\"\"\n        logger.info(\"Step 3: Harmonizing units...\")\n        self.df_harmonized = self.df_normalized.copy()\n\n        for col, conv_info in self.CONVERSIONS.items():\n            if col not in self.df_harmonized.columns:\n                continue\n\n            factor = conv_info['factor']\n            min_val, max_val = conv_info['range']\n\n            # Find values outside expected range (likely wrong units)\n            outside_range = (self.df_harmonized[col] < min_val * 0.1) | \\\n                           (self.df_harmonized[col] > max_val * 1.5)\n\n            # Convert those values\n            converted_count = outside_range.sum()\n            if converted_count > 0:\n                self.df_harmonized.loc[outside_range, col] = \\\n                    self.df_harmonized.loc[outside_range, col] * factor\n\n     self.df_clean = self.df_harmonized.dropna()\n        rows_after = len(self.df_clean)\n        removed = rows_before - rows_after\n\n        logger.info(f\"Removed {removed} incomplete records ({removed/rows_before*100:.1f}%)\")\n        return self.df_clean\n\n    def step5_validate_ranges(self):\n        \"\"\"Validate all values are within expected ranges\"\"\"\n        logger.info(\"Step 5: Validating ranges...\")\n        issues = []\n\n        for col in self.df_clean.columns:\n            if col == self.id_column:\n                continue\n\n            if col in self.REFERENCE_RANGES:\n                min_val, max_val = self.REFERENCE_RANGES[col]\n                outside = ((self.df_clean[col] < min_val) | (self.df_clean[col] > max_val)).sum()\n\n                if outside > 0:\n                    logger.warning(f\"{col}: {outside} values outside range ({min_val}-{max_val})\")\n                    issues.append(f\"{col}: {outside} outliers\")\n\n self.step1_load_data()\n            self.step2_normalize_formats()\n            self.step3_harmonize_units()\n            self.step4_remove_incomplete()\n            self.step5_validate_ranges()\n            self.step6_format_output()\n            self.step7_save_output()\n\n            logger.info(\"=\"*60)\n            logger.info(\"Harmonization Complete!\")\n            logger.info(\"=\"*60)\n **Generate quality report** with statistics\n8. **Preserve patient privacy** in logs\n\n## Common Issues and Solutions\n\n| Issue | Solution |\n|-------|----------|\n| Mixed units in same column | Use range-based detection and conversion |\n| Scientific notation not converting | Ensure string-to-float conversion |\n| Commas not replaced | Use regex=True in replace |\n| Values still out of range |
